theory e2eqss

begin

// Function signature and definition of the equational theory E

functions: adec/2, aenc/2, fst/1, h/1, kdf/1, pair/2, pk/1, sdec/2,
           senc/2, sign/2, snd/1, true/0, verify/3
equations:
    adec(aenc(x.1, pk(x.2)), x.2) = x.1,
    fst(<x.1, x.2>) = x.1,
    sdec(senc(x.1, x.2), x.2) = x.1,
    snd(<x.1, x.2>) = x.2,
    verify(sign(x.1, x.2), x.1, pk(x.2)) = true

rule (modulo E) Register_CA:
   [ Fr( ~skCA ) ]
  --[ OnlyOnce( 'CA' ) ]->
   [ !CA( ~skCA ), !Pk( 'CA', pk(~skCA) ), Out( pk(~skCA) ) ]

  /* has exactly the trivial AC variant */

rule (modulo E) Register_ltk:
   [ !CA( skCA ), Fr( ~kem ) ]
  --[ OnlyOnce( $A ) ]->
   [
   !Ltk( $A, ~kem ), !Pk( $A, pk(~kem) ),
   !Cert( $A, sign(<'cert', $A, pk(~kem)>, skCA) ), Out( pk(~kem) )
   ]

  /* has exactly the trivial AC variant */

rule (modulo E) Reveal_ltk:
   [ !Ltk( $A, kem ) ] --[ LtkReveal( $A ) ]-> [ Out( kem ) ]

  /* has exactly the trivial AC variant */

rule (modulo E) Reveal_kem_secret:
   [ !KemSecret( s ) ] --[ KemReveal( s ) ]-> [ Out( s ) ]

  /* has exactly the trivial AC variant */

rule (modulo E) Reveal_dh_secret:
   [ !DhSecret( s ) ] --[ DhReveal( s ) ]-> [ Out( s ) ]

  /* has exactly the trivial AC variant */

rule (modulo E) MC_1:
   [
   !Ltk( $MC, kemMC ), !Cert( $MC, certMC ), !Pk( $SAT, pkSAT ),
   Fr( ~eph_sk ), Fr( ~dh_sk ), Fr( ~ss_ltSAT ), Fr( ~sid )
   ]
  --[ StartMC( $MC, $SAT, ~sid ) ]->
   [
   St_MC_1( $MC, $SAT, ~sid, kemMC, certMC, ~eph_sk, pk(~eph_sk), ~dh_sk,
            pk(~dh_sk), pkSAT, ~ss_ltSAT, aenc(~ss_ltSAT, pkSAT)
   ),
   Out( <'M1', certMC, pk(~eph_sk), pk(~dh_sk), aenc(~ss_ltSAT, pkSAT)> )
   ]

  /* has exactly the trivial AC variant */

rule (modulo E) SAT_1:
   [
   !Ltk( $SAT, kemSAT ), !Cert( $SAT, certSAT ), !Pk( $MC, pkMC ),
   !Pk( 'CA', pkCA ), In( <'M1', certMC, eph_pk, dh_pk, c_ltSAT> ),
   Fr( ~ss_eph ), Fr( ~ss_dh ), Fr( ~ss_ltMC ), Fr( ~sidS )
   ]
  --[
  Eq( verify(certMC, <'cert', $MC, pkMC>, pkCA), true ),
  Running( $SAT, $MC,
           <'SATtoMC', 
            h(<certMC, certSAT, eph_pk, dh_pk, c_ltSAT, aenc(~ss_eph, eph_pk), 
               aenc(~ss_dh, dh_pk), aenc(~ss_ltMC, pkMC)>)
           >
  ),
  RunningKey( $SAT, $MC,
              kdf(<adec(c_ltSAT, kemSAT), ~ss_eph, ~ss_dh, ~ss_ltMC, 
                   h(<certMC, certSAT, eph_pk, dh_pk, c_ltSAT, aenc(~ss_eph, eph_pk), 
                      aenc(~ss_dh, dh_pk), aenc(~ss_ltMC, pkMC)>)
                  >)
  ),
  DhClaim( ~ss_dh ), KemClaim( ~ss_eph ), Honest( $SAT ), Honest( $MC )
  ]->
   [
   St_SAT_1( $SAT, $MC, ~sidS,
             kdf(<adec(c_ltSAT, kemSAT), ~ss_eph, ~ss_dh, ~ss_ltMC, 
                  h(<certMC, certSAT, eph_pk, dh_pk, c_ltSAT, aenc(~ss_eph, eph_pk), 
                     aenc(~ss_dh, dh_pk), aenc(~ss_ltMC, pkMC)>)
                 >),
             h(<certMC, certSAT, eph_pk, dh_pk, c_ltSAT, aenc(~ss_eph, eph_pk), 
                aenc(~ss_dh, dh_pk), aenc(~ss_ltMC, pkMC)>)
   ),
   !KemSecret( ~ss_eph ), !KemSecret( ~ss_ltMC ), !DhSecret( ~ss_dh ),
   Out( <'M2', certSAT, aenc(~ss_eph, eph_pk), aenc(~ss_dh, dh_pk), 
         aenc(~ss_ltMC, pkMC), 
         senc(<'sc', 
               h(<certMC, certSAT, eph_pk, dh_pk, c_ltSAT, aenc(~ss_eph, eph_pk), 
                  aenc(~ss_dh, dh_pk), aenc(~ss_ltMC, pkMC)>)
              >,
              kdf(<adec(c_ltSAT, kemSAT), ~ss_eph, ~ss_dh, ~ss_ltMC, 
                   h(<certMC, certSAT, eph_pk, dh_pk, c_ltSAT, aenc(~ss_eph, eph_pk), 
                      aenc(~ss_dh, dh_pk), aenc(~ss_ltMC, pkMC)>)
                  >))
        >
   )
   ]

  /*
  rule (modulo AC) SAT_1:
     [
     !Ltk( $SAT, kemSAT ), !Cert( $SAT, certSAT ), !Pk( $MC, pkMC ),
     !Pk( 'CA', pkCA ), In( <'M1', certMC, eph_pk, dh_pk, c_ltSAT> ),
     Fr( ~ss_eph ), Fr( ~ss_dh ), Fr( ~ss_ltMC ), Fr( ~sidS )
     ]
    --[
    Eq( z.1, true ),
    Running( $SAT, $MC,
             <'SATtoMC', 
              h(<certMC, certSAT, eph_pk, dh_pk, c_ltSAT, aenc(~ss_eph, eph_pk), 
                 aenc(~ss_dh, dh_pk), aenc(~ss_ltMC, pkMC)>)
             >
    ),
    RunningKey( $SAT, $MC,
                kdf(<z, ~ss_eph, ~ss_dh, ~ss_ltMC, 
                     h(<certMC, certSAT, eph_pk, dh_pk, c_ltSAT, aenc(~ss_eph, eph_pk), 
                        aenc(~ss_dh, dh_pk), aenc(~ss_ltMC, pkMC)>)
                    >)
    ),
    DhClaim( ~ss_dh ), KemClaim( ~ss_eph ), Honest( $SAT ), Honest( $MC )
    ]->
     [
     St_SAT_1( $SAT, $MC, ~sidS,
               kdf(<z, ~ss_eph, ~ss_dh, ~ss_ltMC, 
                    h(<certMC, certSAT, eph_pk, dh_pk, c_ltSAT, aenc(~ss_eph, eph_pk), 
                       aenc(~ss_dh, dh_pk), aenc(~ss_ltMC, pkMC)>)
                   >),
               h(<certMC, certSAT, eph_pk, dh_pk, c_ltSAT, aenc(~ss_eph, eph_pk), 
                  aenc(~ss_dh, dh_pk), aenc(~ss_ltMC, pkMC)>)
     ),
     !KemSecret( ~ss_eph ), !KemSecret( ~ss_ltMC ), !DhSecret( ~ss_dh ),
     Out( <'M2', certSAT, aenc(~ss_eph, eph_pk), aenc(~ss_dh, dh_pk), 
           aenc(~ss_ltMC, pkMC), 
           senc(<'sc',

                >,
                kdf(<z, ~ss_eph, ~ss_dh, ~ss_ltMC, 
                     h(<certMC, certSAT, eph_pk, dh_pk, c_ltSAT, aenc(~ss_eph, eph_pk), 
                        aenc(~ss_dh, dh_pk), aenc(~ss_ltMC, pkMC)>)
                    >))
          >
     )
     ]
    variants (modulo AC)
    1. $MC   = $MC.35
       c_ltSAT
             = c_ltSAT.41
       certMC
             = certMC.42
       kemSAT
             = kemSAT.46
       pkCA  = pkCA.47
       pkMC  = pkMC.48
       z     = adec(c_ltSAT.41, kemSAT.46)
       z.1   = verify(certMC.42, <'cert', $MC.35, pkMC.48>, pkCA.47)
    
    2. $MC   = $MC.38
       c_ltSAT
             = aenc(z.54, pk(kemSAT.49))
       certMC
             = certMC.45
       kemSAT
             = kemSAT.49
       pkCA  = pkCA.50
       pkMC  = pkMC.51
       z     = z.54
       z.1   = verify(certMC.45, <'cert', $MC.38, pkMC.51>, pkCA.50)
    
    3. $MC   = $MC.114
       c_ltSAT
             = c_ltSAT.120
       certMC
             = sign(<'cert', $MC.114, pkMC.127>, x.226)
       kemSAT
             = kemSAT.125
       pkCA  = pk(x.226)
       pkMC  = pkMC.127
       z     = adec(c_ltSAT.120, kemSAT.125)
       z.1   = true
    
    4. $MC   = $MC.115
       c_ltSAT
             = aenc(z.131, pk(kemSAT.126))
       certMC
             = sign(<'cert', $MC.115, pkMC.128>, x.228)
       kemSAT
             = kemSAT.126
       pkCA  = pk(x.228)
       pkMC  = pkMC.128

rule (modulo E) MC_2:
   [
   St_MC_1( $MC, $SAT, ~sid, kemMC, certMC, ~eph_sk, eph_pk, ~dh_sk, dh_pk,
            pkSAT, ~ss_ltSAT, c_ltSAT
   ),
   !Pk( 'CA', pkCA ), In( <'M2', certSAT, c_eph, c_dh, c_ltMC, conf_s> )
   ]
  --[
  Eq( verify(certSAT, <'cert', $SAT, pkSAT>, pkCA), true ),
  Eq( conf_s,
      senc(<'sc', 
            h(<certMC, certSAT, eph_pk, dh_pk, c_ltSAT, c_eph, c_dh, c_ltMC>)>,
           kdf(<~ss_ltSAT, adec(c_eph, ~eph_sk), adec(c_dh, ~dh_sk), 
                adec(c_ltMC, kemMC), 
                h(<certMC, certSAT, eph_pk, dh_pk, c_ltSAT, c_eph, c_dh, c_ltMC>)>))
  ),
  Commit( $MC, $SAT,
          <'MCtoSAT', 
           h(<certMC, certSAT, eph_pk, dh_pk, c_ltSAT, c_eph, c_dh, c_ltMC>)>
  ),
  AcceptMC( $MC, $SAT,
            kdf(<~ss_ltSAT, adec(c_eph, ~eph_sk), adec(c_dh, ~dh_sk), 
                 adec(c_ltMC, kemMC), 
                 h(<certMC, certSAT, eph_pk, dh_pk, c_ltSAT, c_eph, c_dh, c_ltMC>)>)
  ),
  Secret( kdf(<~ss_ltSAT, adec(c_eph, ~eph_sk), adec(c_dh, ~dh_sk), 
               adec(c_ltMC, kemMC), 
               h(<certMC, certSAT, eph_pk, dh_pk, c_ltSAT, c_eph, c_dh, c_ltMC>)>)
  ),
  Honest( $MC ), Honest( $SAT )
  ]->
   [
   Out( <'M3', 
         senc(<'mc', 
               h(<certMC, certSAT, eph_pk, dh_pk, c_ltSAT, c_eph, c_dh, c_ltMC>)>,
              kdf(<~ss_ltSAT, adec(c_eph, ~eph_sk), adec(c_dh, ~dh_sk), 
                   adec(c_ltMC, kemMC), 
                   h(<certMC, certSAT, eph_pk, dh_pk, c_ltSAT, c_eph, c_dh, c_ltMC>)>))
        >
   ),
   !SessKey( $MC, $SAT,
             kdf(<~ss_ltSAT, adec(c_eph, ~eph_sk), adec(c_dh, ~dh_sk), 
                  adec(c_ltMC, kemMC), 
                  h(<certMC, certSAT, eph_pk, dh_pk, c_ltSAT, c_eph, c_dh, c_ltMC>)>)
   )
   ]

  /*
  rule (modulo AC) MC_2:
     [
     St_MC_1( $MC, $SAT, ~sid, kemMC, certMC, ~eph_sk, eph_pk, ~dh_sk, dh_pk,

     ),
     !Pk( 'CA', pkCA ), In( <'M2', certSAT, c_eph, c_dh, c_ltMC, conf_s> )
     ]
    --[
    Eq( z.3, true ),
    Eq( conf_s,
        senc(<'sc', 
              h(<certMC, certSAT, eph_pk, dh_pk, c_ltSAT, c_eph, c_dh, c_ltMC>)>,
             kdf(<~ss_ltSAT, z, z.1, z.2, 
                  h(<certMC, certSAT, eph_pk, dh_pk, c_ltSAT, c_eph, c_dh, c_ltMC>)>))
    ),
    Commit( $MC, $SAT,
            <'MCtoSAT', 
             h(<certMC, certSAT, eph_pk, dh_pk, c_ltSAT, c_eph, c_dh, c_ltMC>)>
    ),
    AcceptMC( $MC, $SAT,
              kdf(<~ss_ltSAT, z, z.1, z.2, 
                   h(<certMC, certSAT, eph_pk, dh_pk, c_ltSAT, c_eph, c_dh, c_ltMC>)>)
    ),
    Secret( kdf(<~ss_ltSAT, z, z.1, z.2, 
                 h(<certMC, certSAT, eph_pk, dh_pk, c_ltSAT, c_eph, c_dh, c_ltMC>)>)
    ),
    Honest( $MC ), Honest( $SAT )
    ]->
     [
     Out( <'M3', 
           senc(<'mc', 
                 h(<certMC, certSAT, eph_pk, dh_pk, c_ltSAT, c_eph, c_dh, c_ltMC>)>,
                kdf(<~ss_ltSAT, z, z.1, z.2, 
                     h(<certMC, certSAT, eph_pk, dh_pk, c_ltSAT, c_eph, c_dh, c_ltMC>)>))
          >
     ),
     !SessKey( $MC, $SAT,
               kdf(<~ss_ltSAT, z, z.1, z.2, 
                    h(<certMC, certSAT, eph_pk, dh_pk, c_ltSAT, c_eph, c_dh, c_ltMC>)>)

rule (modulo E) SAT_2:
   [ St_SAT_1( $SAT, $MC, ~sidS, MS, th ), In( <'M3', conf_m> ) ]
  --[
  Eq( conf_m, senc(<'mc', th>, MS) ), Commit( $SAT, $MC, <'SATtoMC', th> ),
  AcceptSat( $SAT, $MC, MS ), Secret( MS ), Honest( $SAT ), Honest( $MC )
  ]->
   [ !SessKey( $SAT, $MC, MS ) ]

  /* has exactly the trivial AC variant */

restriction Equality:
  "∀ x y #i. (Eq( x, y ) @ #i) ⇒ (x = y)"
  // safety formula

  /*
  expanded formula:
  "∀ x y #i. (Eq( x, y ) @ #i) ⇒ (x = y)"
  */

restriction OnlyOnce:
  "∀ A #i #j. ((OnlyOnce( A ) @ #i) ∧ (OnlyOnce( A ) @ #j)) ⇒ (#i = #j)"
  // safety formula

  /*
  expanded formula:
  "∀ A #i #j. ((OnlyOnce( A ) @ #i) ∧ (OnlyOnce( A ) @ #j)) ⇒ (#i = #j)"
  */

lemma executable:
  exists-trace
  "∃ MC SAT MS #a #b.
    (AcceptMC( MC, SAT, MS ) @ #a) ∧ (AcceptSat( SAT, MC, MS ) @ #b)"
/*
guarded formula characterizing all satisfying traces:
"∃ MC SAT MS #a #b.
  (AcceptMC( MC, SAT, MS ) @ #a) ∧ (AcceptSat( SAT, MC, MS ) @ #b)"
*/
simplify
solve( St_MC_1( $MC, $SAT, ~sid, kemMC, certMC, ~eph_sk, eph_pk, ~dh_sk,
                dh_pk, pkSAT, ~ss_ltSAT, c_ltSAT
       ) ▶₀ #a )
  case MC_1_case_2
  solve( !Pk( 'CA', pk(x) ) ▶₁ #a )
    case Register_CA
    solve( St_SAT_1( $SAT, $MC, ~sidS,
                     kdf(<~ss_ltSAT, z, z.1, z.2, 
                          h(<sign(<'cert', $MC, pk(~kem)>, ~skCA), 
                             sign(<'cert', $SAT, pk(~kem.1)>, ~skCA), pk(~eph_sk), pk(~dh_sk), 
                             aenc(~ss_ltSAT, pk(~kem.1)), c_eph, c_dh, c_ltMC>)
                         >),
                     th
           ) ▶₀ #b )
      case SAT_1
      solve( !KU( sign(<'cert', $SAT, pk(~kem.1)>, ~skCA) ) @ #vk.3 )
        case MC_1_case_1
        solve( !KU( sign(<'cert', $MC, pk(~kem)>, ~skCA) ) @ #vk.17 )
          case MC_1_case_1
          solve( !KU( senc(<'sc', 
                            h(<sign(<'cert', $MC, pk(~kem)>, ~skCA), 
                               sign(<'cert', $SAT, pk(~kem.1)>, ~skCA), pk(~eph_sk), pk(~dh_sk), 
                               aenc(~ss_ltSAT, pk(~kem.1)), aenc(~ss_eph, pk(~eph_sk)), 
                               aenc(~ss_dh, pk(~dh_sk)), aenc(~ss_ltMC, pk(~kem))>)
                           >,
                           kdf(<~ss_ltSAT, ~ss_eph, ~ss_dh, ~ss_ltMC, 
                                h(<sign(<'cert', $MC, pk(~kem)>, ~skCA), 
                                   sign(<'cert', $SAT, pk(~kem.1)>, ~skCA), pk(~eph_sk), pk(~dh_sk), 
                                   aenc(~ss_ltSAT, pk(~kem.1)), aenc(~ss_eph, pk(~eph_sk)), 
                                   aenc(~ss_dh, pk(~dh_sk)), aenc(~ss_ltMC, pk(~kem))>)
                               >))
                 ) @ #vk.11 )
            case SAT_1
            solve( !KU( senc(<'mc', 
                              h(<sign(<'cert', $MC, pk(~kem)>, ~skCA), 
                                 sign(<'cert', $SAT, pk(~kem.1)>, ~skCA), pk(~eph_sk), pk(~dh_sk), 
                                 aenc(~ss_ltSAT, pk(~kem.1)), aenc(~ss_eph, pk(~eph_sk)), 
                                 aenc(~ss_dh, pk(~dh_sk)), aenc(~ss_ltMC, pk(~kem))>)
                             >,
                             kdf(<~ss_ltSAT, ~ss_eph, ~ss_dh, ~ss_ltMC, 
                                  h(<sign(<'cert', $MC, pk(~kem)>, ~skCA), 
                                     sign(<'cert', $SAT, pk(~kem.1)>, ~skCA), pk(~eph_sk), pk(~dh_sk), 
                                     aenc(~ss_ltSAT, pk(~kem.1)), aenc(~ss_eph, pk(~eph_sk)), 
                                     aenc(~ss_dh, pk(~dh_sk)), aenc(~ss_ltMC, pk(~kem))>)
                                 >))
                   ) @ #vk.15 )
              case MC_2
              solve( !KU( pk(~eph_sk) ) @ #vk.19 )
                case MC_1
                solve( !KU( pk(~dh_sk) ) @ #vk.21 )
                  case MC_1
                  solve( !KU( aenc(~ss_ltSAT, pk(~kem.1)) ) @ #vk.22 )
                    case MC_1
                    solve( !KU( aenc(~ss_eph, pk(~eph_sk)) ) @ #vk.18 )
                      case SAT_1
                      solve( !KU( aenc(~ss_dh, pk(~dh_sk)) ) @ #vk.19 )
                        case SAT_1
                        solve( !KU( aenc(~ss_ltMC, pk(~kem)) ) @ #vk.20 )
                          case SAT_1
                          SOLVED // trace found
                        qed
                      qed
                    qed
                  qed
                qed
              qed
            qed
          qed
        qed
      qed
    qed
  qed
qed

lemma key_secrecy:
  all-traces
  "∀ MS #i.
    (((Secret( MS ) @ #i) ∧ (¬(∃ s #k. KemReveal( s ) @ #k))) ∧
     (¬(∃ s #d. DhReveal( s ) @ #d))) ⇒
    ((¬(∃ #j. K( MS ) @ #j)) ∨
     (∃ A #r. (LtkReveal( A ) @ #r) ∧ (Honest( A ) @ #i)))"
/*
guarded formula characterizing all counter-examples:
"∃ MS #i.
  (Secret( MS ) @ #i)
 ∧
  (∀ s #k. (KemReveal( s ) @ #k) ⇒ ⊥) ∧
  (∀ s #d. (DhReveal( s ) @ #d) ⇒ ⊥) ∧
  (∃ #j. (K( MS ) @ #j)) ∧
  (∀ A #r. (LtkReveal( A ) @ #r) ∧ (Honest( A ) @ #i) ⇒ ⊥)"
*/
simplify
solve( Secret( MS ) @ #i )
  case MC_2
  solve( St_MC_1( $MC, $SAT, ~sid, kemMC, certMC, ~eph_sk, eph_pk, ~dh_sk,
                  dh_pk, pkSAT, ~ss_ltSAT, c_ltSAT
         ) ▶₀ #i )
    case MC_1_case_1
    solve( !Pk( 'CA', pk(x) ) ▶₁ #i )
      case Register_CA
      solve( !KU( kdf(<~ss_ltSAT, z, z.1, z.2, 
                       h(<sign(<'cert', $MC, pk(~kem)>, ~skCA), 
                          sign(<'cert', 'CA', pk(~skCA)>, ~skCA), pk(~eph_sk), pk(~dh_sk), 
                          aenc(~ss_ltSAT, pk(~skCA)), c_eph, c_dh, c_ltMC>)
                      >)
             ) @ #vk )
        case c_kdf
        solve( !KU( ~ss_ltSAT ) @ #vk.13 )
          case MC_1
          by solve( !KU( ~skCA ) @ #vk.21 )
        qed
      qed
    qed
  next
    case MC_1_case_2
    solve( !Pk( 'CA', pk(x) ) ▶₁ #i )
      case Register_CA
      solve( !KU( kdf(<~ss_ltSAT, z, z.1, z.2, 
                       h(<sign(<'cert', $MC, pk(~kem)>, ~skCA), 
                          sign(<'cert', $SAT, pk(~kem.1)>, ~skCA), pk(~eph_sk), pk(~dh_sk), 
                          aenc(~ss_ltSAT, pk(~kem.1)), c_eph, c_dh, c_ltMC>)
                      >)
             ) @ #vk )
        case c_kdf
        solve( !KU( ~ss_ltSAT ) @ #vk.13 )
          case MC_1
          solve( !KU( ~kem.1 ) @ #vk.21 )
            case Reveal_ltk
            by contradiction /* from formulas */
          qed
        qed
      qed
    qed
  qed
next
  case SAT_2
  solve( St_SAT_1( $SAT, $MC, ~sidS, MS, th ) ▶₀ #i )
    case SAT_1_case_1
    solve( splitEqs(1) )
      case split_case_1
      solve( !KU( kdf(<adec(c_ltSAT, ~kem), ~ss_eph, ~ss_dh, ~ss_ltMC, 
                       h(<sign(<'cert', 'CA', pk(~skCA)>, ~skCA), 
                          sign(<'cert', $SAT, pk(~kem)>, ~skCA), eph_pk, dh_pk, c_ltSAT, 
                          aenc(~ss_eph, eph_pk), aenc(~ss_dh, dh_pk), aenc(~ss_ltMC, pk(~skCA))>)
                      >)
             ) @ #vk )
        case c_kdf
        solve( !KU( ~ss_eph ) @ #vk.16 )
          case Reveal_kem_secret
          by contradiction /* from formulas */
        next
          case SAT_1
          solve( !KU( ~ss_dh ) @ #vk.18 )
            case Reveal_dh_secret
            by contradiction /* from formulas */
          next
            case SAT_1
            solve( !KU( ~ss_ltMC ) @ #vk.20 )
              case Reveal_kem_secret
              by contradiction /* from formulas */
            next
              case SAT_1
              by solve( !KU( ~skCA ) @ #vk.24 )
            qed
          qed
        qed
      qed
    next
      case split_case_2
      solve( !KU( kdf(<z, ~ss_eph, ~ss_dh, ~ss_ltMC, 
                       h(<sign(<'cert', 'CA', pk(~skCA)>, ~skCA), 
                          sign(<'cert', $SAT, pk(~kem)>, ~skCA), eph_pk, dh_pk, aenc(z, pk(~kem)), 
                          aenc(~ss_eph, eph_pk), aenc(~ss_dh, dh_pk), aenc(~ss_ltMC, pk(~skCA))>)
                      >)
             ) @ #vk )
        case c_kdf
        solve( !KU( ~ss_eph ) @ #vk.16 )
          case Reveal_kem_secret
          by contradiction /* from formulas */
        next
          case SAT_1
          solve( !KU( ~ss_dh ) @ #vk.18 )
            case Reveal_dh_secret
            by contradiction /* from formulas */
          next
            case SAT_1
            solve( !KU( ~ss_ltMC ) @ #vk.20 )
              case Reveal_kem_secret
              by contradiction /* from formulas */
            next
              case SAT_1
              by solve( !KU( ~skCA ) @ #vk.24 )
            qed
          qed
        qed
      qed
    qed
  next
    case SAT_1_case_2
    solve( splitEqs(1) )
      case split_case_1
      solve( !KU( kdf(<adec(c_ltSAT, ~kem), ~ss_eph, ~ss_dh, ~ss_ltMC, 
                       h(<sign(<'cert', $MC, pk(~kem.1)>, ~skCA), 
                          sign(<'cert', $SAT, pk(~kem)>, ~skCA), eph_pk, dh_pk, c_ltSAT, 
                          aenc(~ss_eph, eph_pk), aenc(~ss_dh, dh_pk), aenc(~ss_ltMC, pk(~kem.1))>)
                      >)
             ) @ #vk )
        case c_kdf
        solve( !KU( ~ss_eph ) @ #vk.16 )
          case Reveal_kem_secret
          by contradiction /* from formulas */
        next
          case SAT_1
          solve( !KU( ~ss_dh ) @ #vk.18 )
            case Reveal_dh_secret
            by contradiction /* from formulas */
          next
            case SAT_1
            solve( !KU( ~ss_ltMC ) @ #vk.20 )
              case Reveal_kem_secret
              by contradiction /* from formulas */
            next
              case SAT_1
              solve( !KU( ~kem.1 ) @ #vk.24 )
                case Reveal_ltk
                by contradiction /* from formulas */
              qed
            qed
          qed
        qed
      qed
    next
      case split_case_2
      solve( !KU( kdf(<z, ~ss_eph, ~ss_dh, ~ss_ltMC, 
                       h(<sign(<'cert', $MC, pk(~kem)>, ~skCA), 
                          sign(<'cert', $SAT, pk(~kem.1)>, ~skCA), eph_pk, dh_pk, 
                          aenc(z, pk(~kem.1)), aenc(~ss_eph, eph_pk), aenc(~ss_dh, dh_pk), 
                          aenc(~ss_ltMC, pk(~kem))>)
                      >)
             ) @ #vk )
        case c_kdf
        solve( !KU( ~ss_eph ) @ #vk.16 )
          case Reveal_kem_secret
          by contradiction /* from formulas */
        next
          case SAT_1
          solve( !KU( ~ss_dh ) @ #vk.18 )
            case Reveal_dh_secret
            by contradiction /* from formulas */
          next
            case SAT_1
            solve( !KU( ~ss_ltMC ) @ #vk.20 )
              case Reveal_kem_secret
              by contradiction /* from formulas */

lemma mutual_authentication_MC [heuristic=O "oracle"]:
  all-traces
  "∀ MC SAT th #i.
    (Commit( MC, SAT, <'MCtoSAT', th> ) @ #i) ⇒
    ((∃ #j. (Running( SAT, MC, <'SATtoMC', th> ) @ #j) ∧ (#j < #i)) ∨
     (∃ A #r. ((LtkReveal( A ) @ #r) ∧ (Honest( A ) @ #i)) ∧ (#r < #i)))"
/*
guarded formula characterizing all counter-examples:
"∃ MC SAT th #i.
  (Commit( MC, SAT, <'MCtoSAT', th> ) @ #i)
 ∧
  (∀ #j. (Running( SAT, MC, <'SATtoMC', th> ) @ #j) ⇒ ¬(#j < #i)) ∧
  (∀ A #r. (LtkReveal( A ) @ #r) ∧ (Honest( A ) @ #i) ⇒ ¬(#r < #i))"
*/
simplify
solve( Commit( MC, SAT, <'MCtoSAT', th> ) @ #i )
  case MC_2
  solve( !KU( senc(<'sc', 
                    h(<certMC, sign(<'cert', $SAT, pkSAT>, x), eph_pk, dh_pk, c_ltSAT, 
                       c_eph, c_dh, c_ltMC>)
                   >,
                   kdf(<~ss_ltSAT, z, z.1, z.2, 
                        h(<certMC, sign(<'cert', $SAT, pkSAT>, x), eph_pk, dh_pk, c_ltSAT, 
                           c_eph, c_dh, c_ltMC>)
                       >))
         ) @ #vk.10 )
    case SAT_1_case_1
    solve( !KU( aenc(~ss_ltSAT, pk(~kem)) ) @ #vk.19 )
      case MC_1
      solve( !KU( aenc(~ss_dh, pk(~dh_sk)) ) @ #vk.10 )
        case SAT_1
        by solve( St_MC_1( $MC, $SAT, ~sid, ~kemMC,
                           sign(<'cert', 'CA', pk(~kemMC)>, ~kemMC), ~eph_sk, pk(~eph_sk), ~dh_sk,
                           pk(~dh_sk), pk(~kem), ~ss_ltSAT, aenc(~ss_ltSAT, pk(~kem))
                  ) ▶₀ #i )
      next
        case c_aenc
        solve( !KU( ~ss_dh ) @ #vk.20 )
          case Reveal_dh_secret
          by solve( St_MC_1( $MC, $SAT, ~sid, ~kemMC,
                             sign(<'cert', 'CA', pk(~kemMC)>, ~kemMC), ~eph_sk, pk(~eph_sk), ~dh_sk,
                             pk(~dh_sk), pk(~kem), ~ss_ltSAT, aenc(~ss_ltSAT, pk(~kem))
                    ) ▶₀ #i )
        next
          case SAT_1

lemma replay_resistance:
  all-traces
  "∀ MC SAT th #i #j.
    ((Commit( MC, SAT, <'MCtoSAT', th> ) @ #i) ∧
     (Commit( MC, SAT, <'MCtoSAT', th> ) @ #j)) ⇒
    (#i = #j)"
/*
guarded formula characterizing all counter-examples:
"∃ MC SAT th #i #j.
  (Commit( MC, SAT, <'MCtoSAT', th> ) @ #i) ∧
  (Commit( MC, SAT, <'MCtoSAT', th> ) @ #j)
 ∧
  ¬(#i = #j)"
*/
simplify
solve( (#i < #j)  ∥ (#j < #i) )
  case case_1
  solve( Commit( MC, SAT, <'MCtoSAT', th> ) @ #i )
    case MC_2
    solve( St_MC_1( $MC, $SAT, ~sid, kemMC, certMC, ~eph_sk, eph_pk, ~dh_sk,
                    dh_pk, pkSAT, ~ss_ltSAT, c_ltSAT
           ) ▶₀ #i )
      case MC_1_case_1
      solve( !Pk( 'CA', pk(x) ) ▶₁ #i )
        case Register_CA
        solve( Commit( $MC, 'CA',
                       <'MCtoSAT', 
                        h(<sign(<'cert', $MC, pk(~kem)>, ~skCA),

lemma hybrid_secure_if_kem_survives:
  all-traces
  "∀ MS #i.
    (((Secret( MS ) @ #i) ∧ (¬(∃ s #k. KemReveal( s ) @ #k))) ∧
     (¬(∃ A #r. (LtkReveal( A ) @ #r) ∧ (Honest( A ) @ #i)))) ⇒
    (¬(∃ #j. K( MS ) @ #j))"
/*
guarded formula characterizing all counter-examples:
"∃ MS #i.
  (Secret( MS ) @ #i)
 ∧
  (∀ s #k. (KemReveal( s ) @ #k) ⇒ ⊥) ∧
  (∀ A #r. (LtkReveal( A ) @ #r) ∧ (Honest( A ) @ #i) ⇒ ⊥) ∧
  (∃ #j. (K( MS ) @ #j))"
*/
simplify
solve( Secret( MS ) @ #i )
  case MC_2
  solve( St_MC_1( $MC, $SAT, ~sid, kemMC, certMC, ~eph_sk, eph_pk, ~dh_sk,
                  dh_pk, pkSAT, ~ss_ltSAT, c_ltSAT
         ) ▶₀ #i )
    case MC_1_case_1
    solve( !Pk( 'CA', pk(x) ) ▶₁ #i )
      case Register_CA
      solve( !KU( kdf(<~ss_ltSAT, z, z.1, z.2, 
                       h(<sign(<'cert', $MC, pk(~kem)>, ~skCA),
